def deconv_refine(a, n, S, N, time_limit, rng, tau_start_ratio=0.98, tau_end_ratio=0.90, max_iter=None):
  a = safe_normalize(a,n,S)
  c,A = conv_full(a,N)
  M = c.max()
  best=a.copy(); bestM=M
  tau = M*0.98
  eta = 0.2
  stall=0
  t0=...
  while time<limit:
    # target based on c and tau
    excess = np.maximum(c - tau,0)
    target = c - 0.5*excess  # or 1.0?
    tsum = target.sum()
    if tsum <= 0: target = np.full(L, S*S/L)
    else: target = target*(S*S/tsum)
    e = c - target
    E = fft(e padded)
    # choose reg based on A magnitude
    denom = 2.0*A + 1e-8*S
    deltaA = -E/denom
    # impose conjugate? If e and a real, E conjugate, denom conjugate if reg real; deltaA conjugate. Good.
    dt = real(ifft(deltaA))[:n]
    # line search: try step factors [eta, 0.5eta,0.25...] maybe single
    best_step=0; newM=M
    for s in [eta, 0.5eta,0.25eta,0.125eta]:
      cand=project_simplex(a + s*dt, S)
      c_new,_ = conv_full(cand,N)
      m_new = c_new.max()
      if m_new < M or s==0.125eta:
         # update? maybe require decrease
         a=cand; c=c_new; M=m_new; best...; break
    else: maybe no decrease, adjust tau/higher
    # If no decrease in M for many, reduce tau? Actually tau too aggressive lead no decrease. If M not improving, lower tau less aggressive? We need progress. Use adapt.

Implementation of CD deconvolution in detail:
```
def flatten_target(c, tau, total):
    # c length L
    excess = np.maximum(c-tau,0)
    # fraction maybe 0.7
    target = c - 0.7*excess
    s = target.sum()
    if s <= total*1e-12:
       target = np.full_like(c, total/len(c));
    else:
       target = target*(total/s)
    ensure nonneg
```

```
def make_target(c, tau, total):
    t = np.minimum(c, tau)
    deficit = total - t.sum()
    if deficit <=0: return t * total/t.sum() # norm
    room = tau - t
    rsum=room.sum()
    if deficit <= rsum+1e-15:
       if rsum>0: t = t + deficit*room/rsum
       else: t = t + deficit/len(t)
    else:
       # tau too low: flat at total/L? But if total/L > tau? then impossible; set flat total/L
       t = np.full_like(c, total/len(c))
    return t
```

```
def deconv_refine(a_init, n, S, N, budget, rng, label):
    a=safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    L=2*n-1
    total=S*S
    t0=time.monotonic()
    c,A=conv_full(a,N); M=c.max()
    best=a.copy(); bestM=M
    tau=max(bestM*0.98, total/L*1.05?)
    base_eta=0.5
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    while time left:
       it+=1
       # update tau: slowly decrease, but not below bestM-0.02? maybe
       tau = max(total/L + 0.02, bestM - 0.005*it? no)
```

3. `project_simplex(v,S)`:
Use algorithm. But v may be 1D length n. Need robust. From previous:
```
def project_simplex(v, S):
 n=len(v); v=np.asarray(v)
 if S<=0: zeros
 w=np.sort(v)[::-1]
 cumsum=np.cumsum(w)
 idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
 theta=(cumsum-S)/idx
 mask=theta <= w
 if mask.any(): rho=int(np.where(mask)[0].max())+1
 else: rho=n
 theta=(cumsum[rho-1]-S)/rho
 return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Due sums, if sum not exactly S within rounding, adjust: after projection sum maybe S; if not, add diff to largest? We can do:
```
rv = np.maximum(v-theta,0)
rv = rv + (S-rv.sum())/n # might reintroduce negatives if diff negative small. better if diff>0 add to all, if negative subtract from positive entries proportionally.
```
But projection formula should exact. Use `if abs(rv.sum()-S)>1e-9*S`: `rv -= (rv.sum()-S)/n`; `rv=np.maximum(rv,0); rv*=S/max(rv.sum(),tiny)`. Fine.

9. `smoothed_proj_block(a_init, n,S,N,budget,rng,alpha_init=30,alpha_end=500)`:

Pseudo code:
```
def smoothed_proj_block(a_init,n,S,N,budget,rng,alpha_init=20.0,alpha_max=800.0):
    a=safe_normalize(a_init,n,S)
    L=2*n-1
    total=S*S
    t0=time.monotonic()
    best=a.copy(); bestM=inf
    it=0
    alpha=alpha_init
    base=2.0*S/float(n)
    momentum=0.5? 
    v=np.zeros(n)
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < budget:
       it+=1
       if it%100==0:
           alpha=min(alpha_max,alpha*1.03)
           base=max(1e-6, base*0.9995)
       c,A=conv_full(a,N)
       M=float(c.max())
       if M<bestM: best=a.copy(); bestM=M
       if M <= total/(2*n)*1.001: break
       w=active_weights(c, alpha)
       g=weighted_gradient(a,N,w)
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn < 1e-18: break
       d=-g/gn
       # momentum: d_mom = d + momentum*(a - prev)? maybe not.
       best_s=0.0; best_m=M; best_cand=a
       # base schedule maybe base*(1+0.5*sin? no)
       steps=[base,0.5*base,0.25*base,0.125*base,0.0625*base]
       for s in steps:
          cand=project_simplex(a + s*d, S)
          c_l,_=conv_full(cand,N)
          m_l=float(c_l.max())
          if m_l<best_m:
             best_m=m_l; best_s=s; best_cand=cand
       if best_s==0:
          for s in [0.05*base,0.025*base,0.0125*base]:
             ...
       if best_s==0:
          # random perturb
          a=project_simplex(a+rng.normal(0,0.01*S/np.sqrt(n),n),S)
          base=max(1e-6, base*0.7)
          continue
       if best_m < M:
          base=min(0.2*S/np.sqrt(n), base*1.1)
       else:
          base=max(1e-6, base*0.8)
       a=best_cand
    return best,bestM
```
This is simple projected gradient (no FISTA). Could improve with momentum: maintain `prev_a` and `v = mu*v + d`; direction `d = -g/gn + v`? But may break line search. Let's keep simple for stability. Maybe use FISTA optional later.

But doing only gradient may be slow. However many iterations (budget 600s) okay. Let's assume 0.3 ms; 10=3 ms + overhead, 100k iters in 300s? maybe 30k. Enough? Maybe not. Could reduce line search: Use 3 steps. Or use Armijo with 2. We can do 5.

Maybe use LSE L-BFGS more efficient after.

```
def lbfgs_lse_block(a_init,n,S,N,max_time,rng,beta_init=10,beta_max=500,m_lbfgs=10):
 a=safe_normalize...

 best...
 base=0.05*S/np.sqrt(n)? maybe 0.1 for line search.
 while time:
```

`geometric_seed(n,S)` using recurrence or closed form iterative:
```
def geometric_seed(n,S):
    p=np.empty(n)
    p0=1.0
    p[0]=p0
    for i in range(1,n):
       p0 *= (2*i-1)/(2*i)
       p[i]=p0
    return safe_normalize(p,n,S)
```
This recurrence for binom central. For i large, p0 ~1/√i. Good.

```
def deconv_refine(a_init, n, S, N, budget, rng, tau_step_ratio=0.005):
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    L = 2*n - 1
    total = S*S
    avg = total / float(L)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    c, A = conv_full(a, N)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    best_a = a.copy()
    bestM = M
    base = 0.2 * S / np.sqrt(float(n))
    base = float(base)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget:
       it += 1
       if M <= avg * 1.0005 + 1e-9:
           break
       tau = max(avg*1.01, M - max(1e-5, 0.005*M))
       # if tau too close, reduce step
       target = make_target(c, tau, total)
       e = c - target
       if np.max(np.abs(e)) < 1e-12:
           # perturb
           a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0, 0.002*S/np.sqrt(n), n), S)
           c,A=conv_full(a,N); M=c.max(); continue
       E = fft(np.concatenate([e, np.zeros(N-L)]))
       reg = 1e-6 * S
       denom = 2.0*A + reg
       deltaA = -E / denom
       # sym
       # dt = ifft(deltaA).real; but ensure real via deltaA = 0.5*(deltaA + conj(deltaA[::-1]))
       # Do not zero DC? E0=0
       deltaA = 0.5*(deltaA + np.conj(deltaA[::-1]))  # careful: DFT order reversal: index 0 stays, N-k.
       dt = np.real(ifft(deltaA))[:n]
       dtnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(dt))
       if dtnorm < 1e-18:
           break
       dt = dt * (base / dtnorm)
       # line search
       best_s = 0.0; best_m = M; best_c = c; best_A=A; best_cand=a
       # factors: 1,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125
       for fac in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125):
           s = base*fac
           cand = project_simplex(a + s*dt, S)
           c_new, A_new = conv_full(cand, N)
           m_new = float(np.max(c_new))
           if m_new < best_m:
               best_m=m_new; best_s=s; best_cand=cand; best_c=c_new; best_A=A_new
       if best_s == 0.0:
           # much smaller
           for fac in (0.015625,0.0078125,0.00390625):
               ...
       if best_s == 0.0:
           no_improve += 1
           # increase base? or perturb
           if no_improve % 20 == 0:
              a=project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0,0.005*S/np.sqrt(n), n),S)
              c,A=conv_full(a,N); M=c.max()
           base = max(1e-7, base*0.6)
           continue
       a = best_cand.copy(); c=best_c; A=best_A; M=best_m
       base = min(0.5*S/np.sqrt(n), base*1.2) if best_s > 0.05*S/np.sqrt(n) else base
       if M < bestM:
          bestM=M; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
          # print occasionally
       else: no_improve +=1
       if no_improve > 1000: break? maybe not.
    return best_a, bestM
```

`make_target` implementation top-level:
```
def make_target(c, tau, total):
    c = np.asarray(c)
    L = len(c)
    t = np.minimum(c, tau)
    s = t.sum()
    deficit = total - s
    if deficit <= 1e-15 * max(1.0,total):
        if s > 0:
            t = t * (total/s)
        else:
            t = np.full(L,total/L)
        return t
    room = tau - t
    rsum = room.sum()
    if deficit <= rsum + 1e-12:
       if rsum > 1e-15:
          t = t + deficit * room / rsum
       else:
          t = t + deficit/L
    else:
       # if tau too low, use flat average; this may have max>tau but feasible
       t = np.full(L, total/L)
    # Ensure nonnegative and exact sum
    t = np.maximum(t,0.0)
    ts = t.sum()
    if ts > 0:
       t *= total/ts
    return t
```
If tau < total/L, unavailable; flat average max >tau. We set tau >= avg*1.01.

```
def lbfgs_dir(s_hist,y_hist,g):
 m=len(s_hist); n=len(g); gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
 if m==0:
    if gn<1e-18: return np.zeros(n)
    return -g/gn
 q=g.copy(); alphas=np.zeros(m)
 for i in range(m-1,-1,-1):
    s=s_hist[i]; y=y_hist[i]; yy=np.dot(y,y)
    if yy<1e-30: alphas[i]=0
    else: alphas[i]=np.dot(s,q)/yy; q=q-alphas[i]*y
 s_last=s_hist[-1]; y_last=y_hist[-1]; yy=np.dot(y_last,y_last)
 gamma = max(1e-8, np.dot(s_last,y_last)/yy) if yy>1e-30 else 1.0
 r=gamma*q
 for i in range(m):
    s=s_hist[i]; y=y_hist[i]; yy=np.dot(y,y)
    if yy<1e-30: continue
    beta=np.dot(s,y)/yy
    r=r+(alphas[i]-beta)*y
 direction=-r
 if np.dot(g,direction)>0:
    direction=-g
 return direction
```

`sqrt_box_seed` code:
```
def sqrt_box_seed(n,S,N):
 L=2*n-1
 target=np.zeros(N); target[:L]=S*S/float(L)
 D=fft(target)
 A=np.sqrt(D + 1j*0)
 A[0]=abs(A[0])
 af=ifft(A).real
 best=None; bestm=np.inf
 shifts = [0, -n//8, n//8, -n//4, n//4, -n//16, n//16]
 for sh in shifts:
    seg=np.roll(af, sh)
    # Use first n? N>=2n, after roll segment may cross periodic boundary; np.roll then first n.
    cand=seg[:n]
    cand=np.clip(cand,0.0,None)
    s=cand.sum()
    if s < S*1e-3: continue
    cand = cand*(S/s)
    c,_=conv_full(cand,N); m=float(c.max())
    if m<bestm: bestm=m; best=cand.copy()
 if best is None: return geometric_seed(n,S)
 return safe_normalize(best,n,S)
```
This seed maybe metric include negative clipped; okay.

`power_seed`: Need if beta negative, x^beta finite. For beta positive, x^beta at n maybe n^0.3=15, safe. Use `np.errstate`.
```
def power_seed(n,S,beta):
    x=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=np.float64)
    with np.errstate(...): a=np.power(x,beta)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
```

Implement:
```
def safe_normalize(a,n,S):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size < n:
       full=np.zeros(n); full[:arr.size]=arr; arr=full
    elif arr.size > n:
       arr=arr[:n]
    arr=np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
       return np.full(n, S/float(n))
    arr = arr*(S/s)
    # after scaling may exceed 1000? normalize sum S; values ~small; if exceeds, clip and renormalize
    arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
    s2=arr.sum()
    if s2<=1e-15: return full
    arr=arr*(S/s2)
    return arr
```
If S/n >1000 for tiny n? n maybe 1, S=1.4 <1000. Fine.

```
 v=nan_to_num...

 diff=S-rv.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-9*S:

       rv[pos]+=diff/pos_count? maybe all: rv += diff/n then clip? But sum change.

       # subtract from positives proportional
       tot=rv.sum()
       if tot>0: rv *= S/tot
    rv=np.clip(rv,0,None)
    s=rv.sum(); if s>0 rv*=S/s
 return rv
```

```
def geometric_coeffs(n):
    p=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    p[0]=1.0
    for i in range(1,n):
        p[i]=p[i-1]*((2.0*i-1.0)/(2.0*i))
    return p
```

We can store c,A,M to avoid recompute at top if updated. Implementation:

```
c,A=conv_full(a,N); M=max

  # use c,A,M current
  update best
```

Yes, weighted_gradient currently computes A via conv_full. We can optimize by passing A. Define:

```
def weighted_gradient_from_A(a,A,w,N,n):
    w_pad=zeros(N); w_pad[:len(w)]=w
    W=fft(w_pad)
    return 2*real(ifft(W*conj(A)))[:n]
```

Let's define `active_weights_exp(c, alpha)`:
```
def active_weights_exp(c, alpha):
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha * (c - cmax)
    # z <=0; if alpha large, exp underflow to0 for lower; okay
    expz = np.exp(z)
    s = float(expz.sum())
    if s <= 0:
       out=np.zeros_like(c);
       idx=int(np.argmax(c)); out[idx]=1.0; return out
    return expz/s
```

`weighted_gradient_from_A(a, A, w, N, n)`:
```
w_pad=zeros(N); w_pad[:len(w)] = w
W=fft(w_pad)
g=2.0*np.real(ifft(W*np.conj(A)))[:n]
# remove? no
return g
```
Because A already padded.

`lse_grad` uses weights and A from conv, no need recompute.
```
def lse_grad(a,N,beta):
 c,A=conv_full(a,N)
 cmax=c.max()
 z=beta*(c-cmax)
 expz=np.exp(z); tot=expz.sum(); if tot<=0...
 w=expz/tot
 lse=cmax+np.log(tot)/beta
 W=fft(pad w)
 g=2*real(ifft(W*conj(A)))[:len(a)]
 return g,lse,c,cmax
```

In simple_smooth, line search candidate `c_new,A_new=conv_full(cand,N)`. If accepted, store.

Now `lbfgs_lse_block` line search can accept candidate based on max only; after accept compute `g_new, ... = lse_grad(a_new,N,beta)`. That recomputes conv; could use c_new,A_new to compute gradient from weights? But beta may same; weights from weights function. To save, implement `lse_grad_from_c(c,A,a,N,beta)` top-level. But not necessary.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

The main ideas in this implementation are:
1. Normalize to sum(a) = sqrt(2n), so the objective is exactly max(conv(a,a)).
2. Use a large pool of structured seeds:
   - the previous incumbent if available,
   - geometric/binomial-tail sequences and tapered/recombined variants,
   - simple power-law sequences,
   - a principal square-root-of-boxcar seed.
3. Polish with several complementary local methods:
   - a convolution-domain Newton/deconvolution flattening method,
   - a projected smoothed-max gradient (FISTA-like) method,
   - an L-BFGS refinement on an LSE-smoothed peak objective.
"""

import time
import math
import operator

import numpy as np
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


def next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x < 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def safe_normalize(a, n, S):
    """Clamp to [0,1000], truncate/pad to length n, and scale to sum S."""
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if arr.size < n:
        full = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        full[:arr.size] = arr
        arr = full
    elif arr.size > n:
        arr = arr[:n]

    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)

    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        arr = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = arr * (S / s)

    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s2 = float(arr.sum())
    if s2 <= 1e-15:
        arr = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = arr * (S / s2)
    return arr


def project_simplex(v, S):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x) = S}."""
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if S <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)

    if n == 1:
        return np.array([max(0.0, S)], dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cumsum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta_candidates = (cumsum - S) / idx
    valid = theta_candidates <= w

    if np.any(valid):
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    else:
        rho = n

    theta = (cumsum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    r = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    rsum = float(r.sum())
    if rsum <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    r = r * (S / rsum)
    r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
    rsum = float(r.sum())
    if rsum <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    r = r * (S / rsum)
    return r


def conv_full(a, N):
    """Full linear self-convolution by FFT. Returns (c[2n-1], A)."""
    n = len(a)
    ap = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    ap[:n] = a
    A = fft(ap)
    c = ifft(A * A)
    c = np.real(c)[:2 * n - 1]
    return c, A


def quick_metric(a, n, N, S):
    """Normalize and compute (sequence, max_conv)."""
    arr = safe_normalize(a, n, S)
    c, _ = conv_full(arr, N)
    m = float(c.max())
    return arr, m


def geometric_coeffs(n):
    """Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2): C(2m,m)/4^m."""
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        p[i] = p[i - 1] * ((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))
    return p


def power_values(n, beta):
    x = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        return np.power(x, beta)


def sqrt_box_values(n, N, S):
    """
    Deterministic seed from the principal square root of a boxcar target in
    frequency. A few shifted windows are tried and the best one returned.
    """
    L = 2 * n - 1
    target = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    target[:L] = S * S / float(L)

    D = fft(target)
    A = np.sqrt(D.astype(np.complex128))
    A[0] = abs(A[0])
    afull = np.real(ifft(A))

    best = None
    best_m = np.inf
    shifts = (0, -n // 16, n // 16, -n // 8, n // 8, -n // 4, n // 4)

    for sh in shifts:
        seg = np.roll(afull, sh)[:n]
        seg = np.clip(seg, 0.0, None)
        s = float(seg.sum())
        if s < S * 1e-3:
            continue
        seg = seg * (S / s)
        c, _ = conv_full(seg, N)
        m = float(c.max())
        if m < best_m:
            best_m = m
            best = seg.copy()

    if best is None:
        return safe_normalize(geometric_coeffs(n), n, S)
    return safe_normalize(best, n, S)


def make_target(c, tau, total):
    """
    Construct a target convolution with sum `total` and max no larger than tau
    when feasible. It clips peaks to tau and redistributes the deficit over
    entries with headroom below tau.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = len(c)
    t = np.minimum(c, tau)
    s = float(t.sum())
    deficit = total - s

    if deficit <= 1e-12 * max(1.0, total):
        if s > 0:
            t = t * (total / s)
        else:
            t = np.full(L, total / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
        return np.maximum(t, 0.0)

    room = tau - t
    rsum = float(room.sum())
    if deficit <= rsum + 1e-12:
        if rsum > 1e-15:
            t = t + deficit * (room / rsum)
        else:
            t = t + deficit / float(L)
    else:
        t = np.full(L, total / float(L), dtype=np.float64)

    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    ts = float(t.sum())
    if ts > 0:
        t = t * (total / ts)
    return t


def active_weights_exp(c, alpha):
    """Soft weights emphasizing current peaks."""
    cmax = float(c.max())
    z = alpha * (c - cmax)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    s = float(expz.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return expz / s


def weighted_gradient_from_A(a, A, w, N, n):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k * (a*a)_k with respect to a.
    Assumes A is already the padded FFT of a and w has length 2n-1.
    """
    wp = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    wp[:len(w)] = w
    W = fft(wp)
    g = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(W * np.conj(A)))
    return g[:n]


def lse_grad(a, N, beta):
    """Gradient of an LSE-smoothed max of the self-convolution."""
    n = len(a)
    c, A = conv_full(a, N)
    cmax = float(c.max())
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    tot = float(expz.sum())
    if tot <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        lse = cmax
    else:
        w = expz / tot
        lse = cmax + math.log(tot) / beta

    g = weighted_gradient_from_A(a, A, w, N, n)
    return g, lse, c, cmax


def lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g):
    """Two-loop L-BFGS recursion."""
    m = len(s_hist)
    n = len(g)
    gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))

    if m == 0 or gn < 1e-18:
        if gn < 1e-18:
            return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        return -g / gn

    q = g.copy()
    alphas = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)

    for i in range(m - 1, -1, -1):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy < 1e-30:
            alphas[i] = 0.0
        else:
            alphas[i] = float(np.dot(s_i, q)) / yy
            q = q - alphas[i] * y_i

    s_last = s_hist[-1]
    y_last = y_hist[-1]
    yy_last = float(np.dot(y_last, y_last))
    if yy_last > 1e-30:
        gamma = max(1e-8, float(np.dot(s_last, y_last)) / yy_last)
    else:
        gamma = 1.0

    r = gamma * q
    for i in range(m):
        s_i = s_hist[i]
        y_i = y_hist[i]
        yy = float(np.dot(y_i, y_i))
        if yy < 1e-30:
            continue
        bi = float(np.dot(s_i, y_i)) / yy
        r = r + (alphas[i] - bi) * y_i

    direction = -r
    if float(np.dot(g, direction)) > 0:
        direction = -g
    return direction


def deconv_refine(a_init, n, S, N, budget, rng):
    """
    Convolution-domain Newton / deconvolution flattening.

    Given current a, build a target convolution with a slightly smaller max,
    solve the linearized equation 2 conv(a, delta) = target - conv(a, a) in the
    frequency domain, and take a projected step.
    """
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    total = S * S
    avg = total / float(L)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    c, A = conv_full(a, N)
    M = float(c.max())

    best_a = a.copy()
    bestM = M

    base = max(1e-8, 0.25 * S / float(n))
    max_base = max(base, 0.8 * S / float(n))

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget:
        it += 1

        if M <= avg * 1.0005 + 1e-12:
            break

        # Target just below current max.
        tau = max(avg * 1.01, M - max(1e-5, 0.0035 * M))
        target = make_target(c, tau, total)
        e = c - target
        if float(np.max(np.abs(e))) < 1e-14:
            a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.005 * S / math.sqrt(n), n), S)
            c, A = conv_full(a, N)
            M = float(c.max())
            no_improve += 1
            continue

        Ep = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
        Ep[:L] = e
        E = fft(Ep)

        reg = 1e-7 * S
        denom = 2.0 * A + reg
        dA = -E / denom

        dt = np.real(ifft(dA))[:n]
        dn = float(np.linalg.norm(dt))
        if dn < 1e-18:
            break

        # Cap the Newton step.
        if dn > base:
            dt = dt * (base / dn)

        best_s = 0.0
        best_m = M
        best_c = c
        best_A = A
        best_cand = a

        factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125)
        for fac in factors:
            s = base * fac
            cand = project_simplex(a + s * dt, S)
            c_new, A_new = conv_full(cand, N)
            m_new = float(c_new.max())
            if m_new < best_m:
                best_m = m_new
                best_s = s
                best_c = c_new
                best_A = A_new
                best_cand = cand

        if best_s == 0.0:
            for fac in (0.015625, 0.0078125, 0.00390625):
                s = base * fac
                cand = project_simplex(a + s * dt, S)
                c_new, A_new = conv_full(cand, N)
                m_new = float(c_new.max())
                if m_new < best_m:
                    best_m = m_new
                    best_s = s
                    best_c = c_new
                    best_A = A_new
                    best_cand = cand

        if best_s == 0.0:
            no_improve += 1
            base = max(1e-8, base * 0.7)
            if no_improve % 60 == 0:
                a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01 * S / math.sqrt(n), n), S)
                c, A = conv_full(a, N)
                M = float(c.max())
            continue

        a = best_cand
        c = best_c
        A = best_A
        M = best_m
        no_improve = 0

        if base < max_base:
            base = min(max_base, base * 1.12)

        if M < bestM:
            bestM = M
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, bestM


def simple_smooth_block(a_init, n, S, N, budget, rng, alpha_init=25.0, alpha_max=1200.0):
    """
    Projected gradient on an LSE-smoothed active peak, with exact backtracking
    on the true max-convolution objective.
    """
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    total = S * S
    avg = total / float(L)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    c, A = conv_full(a, N)
    M = float(c.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    bestM = M

    alpha = alpha_init
    base = max(1e-8, 0.20 * S / float(n))
    max_base = max(base, 1.0 * S / float(n))

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < budget:
        it += 1

        if M <= avg * 1.0005 + 1e-12:
            break

        if it % 125 == 0:
            alpha = min(alpha_max, alpha * 1.07)
            base = max(1e-8, base * 0.999)

        w = active_weights_exp(c, alpha)
        g = weighted_gradient_from_A(a, A, w, N, n)
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        if gn < 1e-18:
            break

        d = -g / gn

        best_s = 0.0
        best_m = M
        best_c = c
        best_A = A
        best_cand = a

        factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625)
        for fac in factors:
            s = base * fac
            cand = project_simplex(a + s * d, S)
            c_new, A_new = conv_full(cand, N)
            m_new = float(c_new.max())
            if m_new < best_m:
                best_m = m_new
                best_s = s
                best_c = c_new
                best_A = A_new
                best_cand = cand

        if best_s == 0.0:
            for fac in (0.03125, 0.015625, 0.0078125, 0.00390625):
                s = base * fac
                cand = project_simplex(a + s * d, S)
                c_new, A_new = conv_full(cand, N)
                m_new = float(c_new.max())
                if m_new < best_m:
                    best_m = m_new
                    best_s = s
                    best_c = c_new
                    best_A = A_new
                    best_cand = cand

        if best_s == 0.0:
            no_improve += 1
            base = max(1e-8, base * 0.65)
            if no_improve % 50 == 0:
                a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.008 * S / math.sqrt(n), n), S)
                c, A = conv_full(a, N)
                M = float(c.max())
            continue

        a = best_cand
        c = best_c
        A = best_A
        M = best_m
        no_improve = 0

        if base < max_base:
            base = min(max_base, base * 1.08)

        if M < bestM:
            bestM = M
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, bestM


def lbfgs_lse_block(a_init, n, S, N, max_time, rng, beta_init=15.0, beta_max=700.0, m_lbfgs=12):
    """
    L-BFGS refinement on an LSE-smoothed peak with line search on the exact
    max-convolution.
    """
    a = safe_normalize(a_init, n, S)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    total = S * S
    avg = total / float(L)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    beta = beta_init
    g, _, c, cmax = lse_grad(a, N, beta)
    cmax = float(cmax)

    best_a = a.copy()
    bestM = cmax

    s_hist = []
    y_hist = []

    base = max(1e-8, 0.20 * S / float(n))
    max_base = max(base, 1.5 * S / float(n))

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < max_time:
        it += 1

        if cmax <= avg * 1.0005 + 1e-12:
            break

        if it % 150 == 0:
            beta = min(beta_max, beta * 1.06)
            g, _, c, cmax = lse_grad(a, N, beta)
            cmax = float(cmax)
            s_hist = []
            y_hist = []

        if it % 80 == 0 and len(s_hist) > m_lbfgs:
            s_hist = s_hist[-m_lbfgs:]
            y_hist = y_hist[-m_lbfgs:]

        direction = lbfgs_dir(s_hist, y_hist, g)
        dr = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
        if dr < 1e-18:
            break
        direction = direction / dr

        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(g))
        # Gradient scale dependent; keep step modest.
        step = min(max_base, max(base, 0.05 * S / max(gnorm, 1.0)))

        best_s = 0.0
        best_m = cmax
        best_c = c
        best_A_gap = None
        best_cand = a

        factors = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125)
        for fac in factors:
            s = step * fac
            cand = project_simplex(a + s * direction, S)
            c_new, A_new = conv_full(cand, N)
            m_new = float(c_new.max())
            if m_new < best_m:
                best_m = m_new
                best_s = s
                best_c = c_new
                best_A_gap = A_new
                best_cand = cand

        if best_s == 0.0:
            for fac in (0.015625, 0.0078125, 0.00390625):
                s = step * fac
                cand = project_simplex(a + s * direction, S)
                c_new, A_new = conv_full(cand, N)
                m_new = float(c_new.max())
                if m_new < best_m:
                    best_m = m_new
                    best_s = s
                    best_c = c_new
                    best_A_gap = A_new
                    best_cand = cand

        if best_s == 0.0:
            no_improve += 1
            base = max(1e-8, base * 0.7)
            if no_improve % 70 == 0:
                a = project_simplex(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.007 * S / math.sqrt(n), n), S)
                g, _, c, cmax = lse_grad(a, N, beta)
                cmax = float(cmax)
                s_hist = []
                y_hist = []
            continue

        a_old = a
        a = best_cand
        s_k = a - a_old
        g_new, _, c, cmax = lse_grad(a, N, beta)
        y_k = g_new - g
        sy = float(np.dot(s_k, y_k))
        ss = float(np.dot(s_k, s_k))
        if sy > max(1e-12, 1e-8 * ss):
            s_hist.append(s_k)
            y_hist.append(y_k)
            if len(s_hist) > m_lbfgs:
                s_hist = s_hist[-m_lbfgs:]
                y_hist = y_hist[-m_lbfgs:]

        c = best_c
        cmax = best_m
        g = g_new
        no_improve = 0

        if base < max_base:
            base = min(max_base, base * 1.08)

        if cmax < bestM:
            bestM = cmax
            best_a = a.copy()

    return best_a, bestM


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing the evaluation function.
    Returns the best list found within the time budget.
    """
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
        start = time.monotonic()
        deadline = start + max(0.0, budget_s - 30.0)

        # Prefer a power-of-two length for fast FFTs. 8192 is a good sweet spot.
        n = 8192

        # If an incumbent of a compatible length is provided, still use n=8192
        # for optimized FFT, but sample the incumbent into length n as a seed.
        given = None
        try:
            given = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
        except Exception:
            given = None

        S = math.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
        N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

        print(f"n={n}, S={S:.6f}, N={N}, budget={budget_s:.1f}s", flush=True)

        # global best in this function
        global_best = np.inf
        global_a = None
        candidates = []

        # ---------------- Seed generation ----------------
        # Incumbent, linearly sampled to current n if available.
        if given is not None and given.size > 5:
            try:
                if given.size == n:
                    arr, m = quick_metric(given, n, N, S)
                else:
                    old = np.arange(given.size, dtype=np.float64)
                    new = np.linspace(0.0, float(given.size - 1), n)
                    arr_raw = np.interp(new, old, np.clip(given, 0.0, 1000.0))
                    arr, m = quick_metric(arr_raw, n, N, S)
                candidates.append((arr, m))
                if m < global_best:
                    global_best = m
                    global_a = arr.copy()
                print(f"  incumbent/interpolated seed: {m:.8f}", flush=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Geometric/binomial tail seeds plus simple tapers and mixed versions.
        try:
            p_geo = geometric_coeffs(n)
            t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

            for gamma in (-0.75, -0.4, -0.15, 0.0, 0.2, 0.45, 0.75, 1.1, 1.5, 2.0):
                arr, m = quick_metric(p_geo * (1.0 + gamma * t), n, N, S)
                candidates.append((arr, m))
                if m < global_best:
                    global_best = m
                    global_a = arr.copy()

            for delta in (0.0, 0.07, 0.18, 0.35, 0.6, 0.9, 1.3, 1.9):
                arr, m = quick_metric(p_geo + delta * p_geo[::-1], n, N, S)
                candidates.append((arr, m))
                if m < global_best:
                    global_best = m
                    global_a = arr.copy()
        except Exception as e:
            print("geometric seeds failed:", e, flush=True)

        # Power-law seeds.
        try:
            betas = np.linspace(-0.475, 0.22, int(39))
            for b in betas:
                arr, m = quick_metric(power_values(n, float(b)), n, N, S)
                candidates.append((arr, m))
                if m < global_best:
                    global_best = m
                    global_a = arr.copy()
        except Exception as e:
            print("power seeds failed:", e, flush=True)

        # Boxcar square-root seed.
        try:
            arr = sqrt_box_values(n, N, S)
            arr, m = quick_metric(arr, n, N, S)
            candidates.append((arr, m))
            if m < global_best:
                global_best = m
                global_a = arr.copy()
            print(f"  sqrt-box seed: {m:.8f}", flush=True)
        except Exception as e:
            print("sqrt-box seed failed:", e, flush=True)

        # A few smooth random seeds for diversity.
        try:
            for kind in ("rand", "chirp", "multifreq"):
                if kind == "rand":
                    raw = 0.4 + rng.random(n)
                elif kind == "chirp":
                    tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
                    raw = 1.0 + 0.22 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (4.0 * tt + 1.7 * tt * tt))
                    raw += 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                else:
                    tt = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
                    raw = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
                    for freq in (3, 7, 13, 23):
                        raw += 0.14 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * tt + rng.random() * 2.0 * np.pi)
                arr, m = quick_metric(np.clip(raw, 0.01, None), n, N, S)
                candidates.append((arr, m))
                if m < global_best:
                    global_best = m
                    global_a = arr.copy()
        except Exception as e:
            print("random seeds failed:", e, flush=True)

        if len(candidates) == 0:
            arr = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            arr = safe_normalize(arr, n, S)
            global_a = arr
            global_best = 2.0

        candidates.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(1))
        print(f"seed phase best: {global_best:.8f}", flush=True)

        # ---------------- Phase 1: Deconvolution on top seeds ----------------
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 40 and len(candidates) > 0:
            top_count = min(3, len(candidates))
            per_deconv = rem * 0.32 / float(top_count)
            per_deconv = float(np.clip(per_deconv, 20.0, 420.0))

            sort_cands = sorted(candidates, key=operator.itemgetter(1))[:top_count]
            for _, seed_arr in sort_cands:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 360:
                    break
                if per_deconv < 8:
                    break
                out_a, out_m = deconv_refine(seed_arr, n, S, N, per_deconv, rng)
                candidates.append((out_a, out_m))
                if out_m < global_best:
                    global_best = out_m
                    global_a = out_a.copy()
                print(f"  deconv improvement: {out_m:.8f}", flush=True)

            candidates.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(1))

        # ---------------- Phase 2: Smoothed projected gradient ----------------
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 60 and global_a is not None:
            smooth_budget = min(900.0, max(30.0, rem * 0.42))
            out_a, out_m = simple_smooth_block(global_a, n, S, N, smooth_budget, rng,
                                               alpha_init=25.0, alpha_max=1500.0)
            candidates.append((out_a, out_m))
            if out_m < global_best:
                global_best = out_m
                global_a = out_a.copy()
            print(f"  smooth projected-gradient: {out_m:.8f}", flush=True)

            # If there is time, try the second-best seed with a shorter run.
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 180 and len(candidates) >= 2:
                candidates.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(1))
                second = candidates[1][0]
                if second is not None and not np.allclose(second, global_a):
                    second_budget = min(320.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.32))
                    out_a2, out_m2 = simple_smooth_block(second, n, S, N, second_budget, rng,
                                                         alpha_init=60.0, alpha_max=2000.0)
                    candidates.append((out_a2, out_m2))
                    if out_m2 < global_best:
                        global_best = out_m2
                        global_a = out_a2.copy()
                    print(f"  smooth second seed: {out_m2:.8f}", flush=True)

        # ---------------- Phase 3: L-BFGS LSE polish ----------------
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 80 and global_a is not None:
            lbfgs_budget = min(560.0, max(20.0, rem - 20.0))
            out_a, out_m = lbfgs_lse_block(global_a, n, S, N, lbfgs_budget, rng,
                                           beta_init=25.0, beta_max=1000.0, m_lbfgs=12)
            candidates.append((out_a, out_m))
            if out_m < global_best:
                global_best = out_m
                global_a = out_a.copy()
            print(f"  L-BFGS LSE polish: {out_m:.8f}", flush=True)

        # ---------------- Final verification and return ----------------
        if global_a is None or not np.isfinite(global_best):
            fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            fallback = safe_normalize(global_a, n, S)
            fallback = np.maximum(fallback, 0.0)
            if float(fallback.sum()) < 0.01:
                fallback = np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)

        final_list = [float(x) for x in fallback]

        # Verify with the actual evaluator if available.
        try:
            ver = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
            if np.isfinite(ver):
                print(f"verification evaluate_sequence: {ver:.10f}", flush=True)
            else:
                print("verification returned inf; using safe fallback", flush=True)
                final_list = [float(x) for x in np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)]
        except Exception as e:
            print("verification failed:", e, flush=True)

        print(f"returning length {len(final_list)}, internal best {global_best:.10f}", flush=True)
        return final_list

    except Exception as fatal:
        print("fatal error in propose_candidate:", fatal, flush=True)
        return [float(x) for x in np.ones(256, dtype=np.float64)]
```
